\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}H_{\rho}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda;\bm{v})-\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}H_{\rho}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda;\bm{v}^{\prime})\leq H_{\rho}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}};\bm{v})-H_{\rho}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}};\bm{v}^{\prime}),

(D.5)

where we utilize the optimality of
\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
under
S^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
and the property of supremum operator.
Now given definition (
D.3
), to prove that the right hand side of (
D.5
) is non-positive, it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}}\leq\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}}.

(D.6)

Now we invoke Lemma
D.2
, which shows that if an item
j
belongs to the optimal assortment
S^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
, then it holds

\displaystyle r_{j}\geq R_{\rho}^{\star}(\bm{v})+\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}}\cdot\rho,

(D.7)

which is equivalent to the following inequality,

\displaystyle\exp(-r_{j}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})\leq\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}}.

(D.8)

Then with (
D.8
), considering the following arguments,

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})+\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}(v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i})\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{+}}v_{i}+\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i}}

(D.9)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})+\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}(v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i})\cdot\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}}}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}+\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i}}

(D.10)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}},

(D.11)

where the inequality follows from (
D.8
) and the last equality is from the equation that
(a+c\cdot(a/b))/(b+c)=a/b
.
This proves (
D.6
) and thus completes the proof of Lemma
D.1
.
∎

Lemma D.2

(Condition for items in optimal robust assortment (Example
2.1
))
.

Consider Example
2.1
with fixed parameter
\bm{v}
. If an item
j\in[N]
belongs to the optimal robust assortment
S^{\star}
, then it holds that

\displaystyle r_{j}\geq R_{\rho}(S^{\star};\bm{v})+\lambda^{\star}\cdot\rho,\quad(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star})\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{S\subseteq[N],|S|\leq K,\lambda\geq 0}H_{\rho}(S,\lambda;\bm{v})\,\,\text{with }j\in S^{\star}.

(D.12)

Proof of Lemma
D.2
.

We prove the conclusion by contradiction.
If there exists an item
j
such that
j\in S^{\star}
for some
(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star})
satisfying

\displaystyle(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star})\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{S\subseteq[N],|S|\leq K,\lambda\geq 0}H_{\rho}(S,\lambda;\bm{v}),

(D.13)

but
r_{j}<R_{\rho}(S^{\star};\bm{v})+\lambda^{\star}\rho
.
Then considering the new assortment
S^{\star}_{-j}:=S^{\star}\setminus\{j\}
which satisfies the cardinality constraint
|S^{\star}_{-j}|\leq K
.
By calculation, we can obtain that
H_{\rho}(S^{\star}_{-j},\lambda^{\star};\bm{v})>H_{\rho}(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star};\bm{v})
, which contradicts (
D.13
).
Thus we must have
r_{j}\geq R_{\rho}(S^{\star};\bm{v})+\lambda^{\star}\rho
.
∎

Lemma D.3

(Monotonicity under optimal robust assortment (Example
2.2
))
.

Consider Example
2.2
and two sets of parameters
\bm{v}
and
\bm{v}^{\prime}
such that
v_{j}\leq v_{j}^{\prime}
for all
j\in[N]
.
Then for the assortment
S^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
defined as

\displaystyle S^{\star}_{\bm{v}}\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{S\subseteq[N],|S|\leq K}\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S,\lambda;\bm{v})\right\},

(D.14)

the following inequality holds,

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda;\bm{v})\right\}\leq\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda;\bm{v}^{\prime})\right\}.

(D.15)

Proof of Lemma
D.3
.

To facilitate presentation, for any parameter
\bm{v}
, we define

\displaystyle(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{S\subseteq[N],|S|\leq K,\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S,\lambda;\bm{v})\right\},

(D.16)

with which we can come to the following upper bound,

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda;\bm{v})\right\}-\sup_{\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda;\bm{v}^{\prime})\right\}\leq\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda_{\bm{v}}^{\star};\bm{v})-\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{\bm{v}},\lambda_{\bm{v}}^{\star};\bm{v}^{\prime}),

(D.17)

where we utilize the optimality of
\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
under
S^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
and the property of supremum operator.
Now given definition (
4.6
) of
\widetilde{H}
, to prove that the right hand side of (
D.17
) is non-positive, it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle-\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}}\right)+\lambda_{\bm{v}}^{\star}\cdot\log\left(e^{\rho_{0}}-\frac{(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}}\right)

(D.18)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq-\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}}\right)+\lambda_{\bm{v}}^{\star}\cdot\log\left(e^{\rho_{0}}-\frac{(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}}\right).

(D.19)

This is further equivalent to proving the following,

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}\leq\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}.

(D.20)

Now we invoke Lemma
D.4
, which shows that for any item
i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle r_{i}\geq-\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v^{\mathrm{tot}})}\right),

(D.21)

which is equivalent to the following inequality,

\displaystyle\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})\leq\frac{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v^{\mathrm{tot}})}.

(D.22)

Then with (
D.22
), we consider the following arguments,

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}^{\prime}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}

(D.23)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})+\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}(v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i})\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}+e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}(v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i})-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}

(D.24)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})+e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}(v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i})\cdot\frac{\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v^{\mathrm{tot}})}}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}+e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}(v_{i}^{\prime}-v_{i})-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}

(D.25)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{\bm{v},+}^{\star}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v^{\mathrm{tot}})},

(D.26)

where the inequality follows from (
D.22
) and the last equality is from the equation that
(a+c\cdot(a/b))/(b+c)=a/b
.
This proves (
D.19
) and thus completes the proof of Lemma
D.3
.
∎

Lemma D.4

(Optimal condition for items in optimal robust assortment (Example
2.2
))
.

Consider Example
2.2
with fixed parameter
\bm{v}
. If an item
j\in[N]
belongs to the robust assortment
S^{\star}
in the following sense,

\displaystyle j\in S^{\star},\quad(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star})\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{S\subseteq[N],|S|\leq K,\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S,\lambda;\bm{v})\right\},

(D.27)

then the following inequality holds,

\displaystyle r_{j}\geq-\lambda^{\star}\cdot\log\left(\frac{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{+}^{\star}}v_{i}\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{+}^{\star}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v^{\mathrm{tot}})}\right).

(D.28)

Proof of Example
D.4
.

The proof follows from a similar strategy as the proof of Lemma
D.2
.
If there exists an item
j
such that
j\in S^{\star}
for some
(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star})
satisfying that

\displaystyle(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star})\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{S\subseteq[N],|S|\leq K,\lambda\geq 0}\left\{\widetilde{H}(S,\lambda;\bm{v})\right\},

(D.29)

but also satisfying that

\displaystyle r_{j}<-\lambda^{\star}\cdot\log\left(\frac{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{+}^{\star}}v_{i}\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S_{+}^{\star}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v^{\mathrm{tot}})}\right).

(D.30)

Then considering the new assortment
S^{\star}_{-j}:=S^{\star}\setminus\{j\}
which satisfies the cardinality constraint
|S^{\star}_{-j}|\leq K
.
The value of
\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{-j},\lambda^{\star};\bm{v})
would be

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{-j},\lambda^{\star};\bm{v})=-\lambda^{\star}\cdot\log\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{-j,+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda)}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{-j,+}}v_{i}}\right)-\lambda^{\star}\rho_{0}+\lambda^{\star}\cdot\log\left(e^{\rho_{0}}-\frac{(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}{\sum_{i\in S_{-j,+}^{\star}}v_{i}}\right).

(D.31)

In the sequel, we are going to show that the above
\widetilde{H}(S^{\star}_{-j},\lambda^{\star};\bm{v})
is larger than

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\widetilde{H}(S^{\star},\lambda^{\star};\bm{v})=-\lambda^{\star}\cdot\log\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda)}{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{+}}v_{i}}\right)-\lambda^{\star}\rho_{0}+\lambda^{\star}\cdot\log\left(e^{\rho_{0}}-\frac{(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}{\sum_{i\in S_{+}^{\star}}v_{i}}\right),

(D.32)

causing a contradiction.
To this end, in view of (
D.31
) and (
D.32
), it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}\setminus\{j\}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}\setminus\{j\}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}\leq\frac{\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}\cdot\exp(-r_{i}/\lambda^{\star}_{\bm{v}})}{e^{\rho_{0}}\cdot\sum_{i\in S^{\star}_{\bm{v},+}}v_{i}-(e^{\rho_{0}}-1)\cdot(1+v_{\mathrm{tot}})}.

(D.33)

By calculations, this is equivalent to the Condition (
D.30
).
Therefore, (
D.30
) can not hold for any
j\in S^{\star}
satisfying (
D.29
). This completes the proof of Lemma
D.4
.
∎

Lemma D.5

(Concentration 1
(
Saha and Gaillard 2024
)
)
.

With probability at least
1-3Ne^{-\delta}
, it holds simultaneously for all
i\in[N]
that
v^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{i}\leq v_{i}
and that one of the following two inequalities is satisfied,

\displaystyle\tau_{i,0}<69\delta\cdot(1+v_{i}),

(D.34)

or

\displaystyle v_{i}-v_{i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\leq 4(1+v_{i})\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2v_{i}\delta}{\tau_{i,0}}}+\frac{22\delta\cdot(1+v_{i})^{2}}{\tau_{i,0}}.

(D.35)

Proof of Lemma
D.5
.

See Lemma 1 in
Saha and Gaillard 2024
for a detailed proof.
∎

Lemma D.6

(Concentration 2)
.

Conditioning on the assortment set
\{S_{k}\}_{k=1}^{n}
and the event that

\displaystyle n_{i}:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i\in S_{k}\}\geq 138\cdot\max\left\{1,\frac{256}{138v_{i}}\right\}\cdot(1+v_{\max})(1+Kv_{\max})\log(3N/\delta),\quad\forall i\in S^{\star},

(D.36)

with probability at least
1-2\delta
, it holds that for any item
i\in[N]
,

\displaystyle v_{i}-v_{i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\leq 8\cdot\sqrt{\frac{v_{i}(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i_{k}\in S_{k}\}\cdot(1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j})^{-1}}}+\frac{44(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i_{k}\in S_{k}\}\cdot(1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j})^{-1}}.

(D.37)

As a corollary, under also the Condition (
D.36
), we have that for any item
i\in[N]
,

\displaystyle v_{i}\leq 2v_{i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}.

(D.38)

Proof of Lemma
D.6
.

The proof of Lemma
D.6
is based upon Lemma
D.5
.
By definition, we have that

\displaystyle\tau_{i,0}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\big\{i_{k}\in\{0,i\}\big\}\cdot\mathbf{1}\big\{i\in S_{k}\big\}.

(D.39)

Notice that conditioning on the assortments
\{S_{k}\}_{k=1}^{n}
, for any sample
k
such that
i\in S_{k}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}\big\{i_{k}\in\{0,i\}\big\}\sim\mathrm{Bernoulli}\left(\frac{1+v_{i}}{1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j}}\right).

(D.40)

Therefore, by applying Chernoff’s bound, we can derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\tau_{i,0}\leq(1-\mu)\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\big\{i\in S_{k}\big\}\cdot\frac{1+v_{i}}{1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j}}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\big\{i\in S_{k}\big\}\cdot\frac{1+v_{i}}{1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j}}\right).

(D.41)

Choosing
\mu=1/2
, applying the Condition (
D.36
), we can derive that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\tau_{i,0}\leq 69(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)\Big)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\tau_{i,0}\leq(1-\mu)\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\big\{i\in S_{k}\big\}\cdot\frac{1+v_{i}}{1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j}}\right)\leq\frac{\delta}{N}.

(D.42)

Therefore, in view of Lemma
D.5
, by applying a union bound argument, with probability at least
1-2\delta
,

\displaystyle v_{i}-v_{i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\leq 8\cdot\sqrt{\frac{v_{i}(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i_{k}\in S_{k}\}\cdot(1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j})^{-1}}}+\frac{44(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i_{k}\in S_{k}\}\cdot(1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j})^{-1}}.

(D.43)

Finally, to prove the corollary, we just need to verify that under Condition (
D.36
),

\displaystyle v_{i}-v_{i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\leq 8\cdot\sqrt{\frac{v_{i}(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i_{k}\in S_{k}\}\cdot(1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j})^{-1}}}+\frac{44(1+v_{i})\log(3N/\delta)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\{i_{k}\in S_{k}\}\cdot(1+\sum_{j\in S_{k}}v_{j})^{-1}}\leq\frac{v_{i}}{2}.

(D.44)

This completes the proof of Lemma
D.6
.
∎

Lemma D.7
.

For any
\lambda\in[c\cdot r_{\max},r_{\max}/\rho]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda\cdot\exp(r_{\max}/\lambda)\cdot\big(1-\exp(-r_{\max}/\lambda)\big)\leq c\cdot r_{\max}\cdot\big(\exp(1/c)-1\big).

(D.45)

Proof of Lemma
D.7
.

We define the following function,

\psi(\lambda):=\lambda\cdot\exp(r_{\max}/\lambda)\cdot(1-\exp(-r_{\max}/\lambda)).

Then we can verify that the derivative of
\psi(\lambda)
is non-positive, i.e.,

\displaystyle\psi^{\prime}(\lambda)=\exp(r_{\max}/\lambda)\cdot(1-r_{\max}/\lambda)-1\leq 0,\quad\forall\lambda>0.

(D.46)

Therefore, we can derive the following result,

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\in[c\cdot r_{\max},r_{\max}/\rho]}\psi(\lambda)=\psi(c\cdot r_{\max})=c\cdot r_{\max}\cdot\big(\exp(1/c)-1\big).

(D.47)

This completes the proof of Lemma
D.7
.
∎

Lemma D.8

(Fano’s lemma)
.

Let
\Gamma:=\{\theta^{1},\dots,\theta^{M}\}\subset\Theta
be a
2\delta
-separated set under some metric
\rho
over
\Theta,
each associated with a distribution
\mathbb{P}_{\theta}
over some set
\mathcal{X}
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\inf_{\pi:\mathcal{X}\mapsto\Theta}\sup_{k\in[M]}{\mathbb{E}}_{D\sim P_{\theta_{k}}}[\rho(\pi(D),\theta_{k})]\geq\delta\cdot\left(1-\frac{\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{M}D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathbb{P}_{\theta_{i}}\lVert\mathbb{P}_{\theta_{j}})+\log 2}{\log M}\right).

Proof of Lemma
D.8
.

Please refer to Proposition 15.12 in
Wainwright 2019
.
∎
